# IRGS Shared Content Library — Full Qualification Companion
## Core v3.2 + SCL v1.0 | Maturation R1+R2 consolidated review | 14 October 2026

**Authority:** PROPOSED SUPPLEMENT. This document is an editorial view of the existing R1/R2 drafts, not a normative replacement for the SCL v1.0 registry. It cannot change `@0.2` reference meaning, instantiate actors/assets, promote a held candidate, or migrate a campaign.

## Navigation and precedence

1. Core procedures: `IRGS_Core_Engine_v3_2.md` — normative.
2. Library overview: `IRGS_Shared_Content_Library_v1_0.md` — normative.
3. Canonical identities, source payloads and gates: `IRGS_Shared_Content_Registry_v1_0.json` — normative.
4. Previously selected definitions: `IRGS_SCL_v1_0_Selected_Contracts.md` — selected qualitative contracts.
5. This companion and unified overlay: proposed qualification only.

## Inventory

| Unit | Count | Status |
|---|---:|---|
| Canonical catalogue families | 279 | Unchanged |
| Already selected qualitative families | 51 | Unchanged |
| Held families, with proposed R1 draft | 36 | Still held |
| Held families, with proposed R2 draft | 192 | Still held |
| Held families, total | 228 | Still held |
| Distinct acceptance case specifications | 420 | Authored, not individually executed in Core runtime |

## Universal qualification rules

- Confirm source identity, typed alias, mechanical adapter and actual release checksum before any replacement.
- A reference does not create stock, power, a person, a skill, an event, an institution, a source of information, or an equipment operation.
- Maintain only one authoritative writer per resource, fact, location, or condition; all views link back to that owner.
- Check prerequisites, actual quantity/capacity, activation, mode, environment, authorization and native performance envelope before effect.
- Record actual timing, costs, resource transfers, useful output, observations, reporting and failure separately.
- Keep a held family held until entry-level cases are executed, numerical/operating parameters are calibrated where consequential, a new immutable version is published, and adoption is explicit.

## Source and qualification matrix

See `IRGS_SCL_Full_Reconciliation_Matrix.csv`, `IRGS_SCL_Qualification_Acceptance_Matrix.csv`, and `IRGS_SCL_Consolidated_Qualification_R2.json` for exact indexed lookups.

---

# PART I — 36 R1 proposed dossiers

The following R1 qualitative contracts are reproduced in compiled form; their original data and text remain separately available in the source R1 Markdown and JSON files.

### SCL:PBC-BIO-FL-BODY-001@0.2 — Humanoid

**Type:** body_plan | **Status:** PROPOSED; source-backed hold remains | **Source:** Head, neck, torso, two arms, two legs — Humanoid function/location mapping; physiology selected by species

**Purpose And Method:** Body organization archetype: Humanoid. Source description: Head, neck, torso, two arms, two legs — Humanoid function/location mapping; physiology selected by species. Not a species, an actor or a universal physiology.

**Activation:** Bind an existing actual body using humanoid geometry, functional organs/components, locomotion, sensing and injury/repair profiles appropriate to that entity.

**Limits And Failure:** A plan does not imply cognition, human organ distribution, environmental tolerance, reproduction, recovery, hit chance or immunity.

**Acceptance:** Two organisms/machines sharing humanoid plan may have distinct injured parts and functions; the type lookup adds no creature or body state.

**Maintenance:** Biological recovery, repair and replacement follow actual physiology or component rules; no free healing or repair.

**Instance Binding:** K-ACTOR or K-ASSET owns the existing organism/construct; the plan is a read-only structural classifier.

**Overrides:** Species-specific anatomy remaps or omits organs and parts explicitly, without rewriting historic injuries.

**Runtime:** Use declared regional/function map only when the selected effect model needs it; no automatic humanoid hit table.

**Portability:** Applies across living and constructed embodiments where the described organization is physically supported.

**Unresolved:** actual anatomy/components; locomotion; physiology/function dependencies; selected location/effect profile if used

**Required qualification:** Trace each claim to source row and native-adapter boundary; Execute documented acceptance counterexample in at least two compatible contexts where relevant; Demonstrate unchanged current aliases and source payload hashes; Publish a new immutable per-definition revision before formally replacing the held gate

### SCL:PBC-MAT-FL-MAT-002@0.2 — Hardwood

**Type:** material | **Status:** PROPOSED; source-backed hold remains | **Source:** stronger structural baseline, heavier/harder to work

**Purpose And Method:** Qualitative material-family descriptor for Hardwood: stronger structural baseline, heavier/harder to work. Material identity does not specify an item, construction or finished-product quality.

**Activation:** Select actual hardwood grade/species/composition when relevant, dimensions, environment, construction method and appropriate material-property evidence before assessing a real component.

**Limits And Failure:** No universal density, tensile strength, resistance, edge performance or mass multiplier is supplied. The family name alone cannot upgrade armor, durability or a weapon effect.

**Acceptance:** Two physically different products may both use Hardwood without being equal in mass, quality, protection or manufacture; changing the reference does not change actual stocks.

**Maintenance:** Repair/degradation arises from actual component, exposure and compatible engineering or craftsmanship profile; no universal wear cycle.

**Instance Binding:** Material descriptor is not a stock lot. An actual piece, item or bulk material needs an owned asset or stock record and measured units.

**Overrides:** A setting may define a special grade with explicit supported properties, but cannot silently rewrite existing components.

**Runtime:** Read material family as a construction/property constraint; defer numerical effects to the selected calibrated material/component profile.

**Portability:** Reusable in historical, survival, fantasy and technical contexts where this material exists, without imposing any era or fantasy physics.

**Unresolved:** grade/composition; relevant physical properties; workability; environmental limits

**Required qualification:** Trace each claim to source row and native-adapter boundary; Execute documented acceptance counterexample in at least two compatible contexts where relevant; Demonstrate unchanged current aliases and source payload hashes; Publish a new immutable per-definition revision before formally replacing the held gate

### SCL:PBC-NET-PL-NET-N-001@0.2 — Settlement Node

**Type:** node | **Status:** PROPOSED; source-backed hold remains | **Source:** Habitation/community participating in exchange — Services, imports/exports, storage, institutions, access

**Purpose And Method:** Network role/projection for an existing settlement node: Habitation/community participating in exchange — Services, imports/exports, storage, institutions, access. The node is an index onto actual sites, populations and services.

**Activation:** Bind an existing referenced location/actor plus its role and actual link interfaces, storage, personnel, authority and settlement node capacities.

**Limits And Failure:** Creating a node projection adds no warehouse, stock, equipment, workforce, route throughput, sovereignty or transport link.

**Acceptance:** A settlement node can appear in two route views without doubling site capacity or stock. No product arrives until the real route and handover complete.

**Maintenance:** Underlying site, network and asset owners manage their actual maintenance and outages; a view has no extra lifecycle.

**Instance Binding:** Node is a non-instantiating role or view; owner refs point to one site/institution/asset truth.

**Overrides:** Role classifications can change prospectively; past recorded transfers and ownership are not reinterpreted.

**Runtime:** Read capacities/access from actual node/site and links; allocate one actual constraint across simultaneous demands.

**Portability:** Works for historical, modern, fantasy and space networks when compatible links and endpoints exist.

**Unresolved:** existing site/community reference; real role; supported flow/handling; access/storage/constraints

**Required qualification:** Trace each claim to source row and native-adapter boundary; Execute documented acceptance counterexample in at least two compatible contexts where relevant; Demonstrate unchanged current aliases and source payload hashes; Publish a new immutable per-definition revision before formally replacing the held gate

### SCL:PBC-NET-PL-NET-N-003@0.2 — Depot/Storage Node

**Type:** node | **Status:** PROPOSED; source-backed hold remains | **Source:** Concentrated storage/distribution point — Stocks, handling, security, access, throughput

**Purpose And Method:** Network role/projection for an existing depot/storage node: Concentrated storage/distribution point — Stocks, handling, security, access, throughput. The node is an index onto actual sites, populations and services.

**Activation:** Bind an existing referenced location/actor plus its role and actual link interfaces, storage, personnel, authority and depot/storage node capacities.

**Limits And Failure:** Creating a node projection adds no warehouse, stock, equipment, workforce, route throughput, sovereignty or transport link.

**Acceptance:** A depot/storage node can appear in two route views without doubling site capacity or stock. No product arrives until the real route and handover complete.

**Maintenance:** Underlying site, network and asset owners manage their actual maintenance and outages; a view has no extra lifecycle.

**Instance Binding:** Node is a non-instantiating role or view; owner refs point to one site/institution/asset truth.

**Overrides:** Role classifications can change prospectively; past recorded transfers and ownership are not reinterpreted.

**Runtime:** Read capacities/access from actual node/site and links; allocate one actual constraint across simultaneous demands.

### SCL:PBC-WPN-FL-COV-01@0.2 — Head

**Type:** coverage | **Status:** PROPOSED; source-backed hold remains | **Source:** COV-01 | Head

**Purpose And Method:** Anatomy-facing protection-region label: Head. Identifies nominal regions, not any physical protective object.

**Activation:** Bind the actual wearer/body plan and precise material component locations before using head as a coverage description.

**Limits And Failure:** Nominal regional coverage is not complete protection and cannot imply thickness, fit, armor value, orientation or adjacent regions.

**Acceptance:** A head coverage tag without a corresponding actual protective component grants no resistance or inventory. Any uncovered subregion remains uncovered.

**Maintenance:** No independent maintenance; physical attached protection is maintained by the item/component owner.

**Instance Binding:** Coverage is a geometry descriptor, never an asset or lot. Modifying coverage does not materialize armor.

**Overrides:** Anatomies lacking the named region must map explicitly or mark the tag not applicable; no forced humanoid hit table.

**Runtime:** Resolve region intersection against current position/anatomy and real component coverage before consulting genre protection mechanics.

**Portability:** Applicable to any genre with compatible anatomy, protective equipment and contact geometry.

**Unresolved:** actual anatomy/region mapping; specific covered parts/gaps

**Required qualification:** Trace each claim to source row and native-adapter boundary; Execute documented acceptance counterexample in at least two compatible contexts where relevant; Demonstrate unchanged current aliases and source payload hashes; Publish a new immutable per-definition revision before formally replacing the held gate

### SCL:PBC-WPN-FL-SHD-001@0.2 — Buckler

**Type:** shield | **Status:** PROPOSED; source-backed hold remains | **Source:** weapon-control, close defense

**Purpose And Method:** Physical protective equipment family: Buckler; source-specified typical purpose: weapon-control, close defense. It requires real size/material/configuration and is not a free defense action.

**Activation:** Bind one existing shield asset, grip/strap or deployment support, actor capability, orientation, protected sector, condition and selected genre protection/stress adapter.

**Limits And Failure:** No universal shield structure, coverage percentage, defense modifier, deflection or free reactive attack is implied. A shield behind the wearer does not protect the front.

**Acceptance:** One buckler appears as one owned asset; changing its defense use/deployed mode cannot create a second shield or protection outside its actual geometry.

**Maintenance:** Stress and repairs update the actually struck or defective component under the selected adapter; the mode choice does not renew condition.

**Instance Binding:** K-ASSET owns the individual shield and condition; K-ACTOR owns available action/commitment; stock only records separate physical lots.

**Overrides:** A setting may alter construction and authorization with explicit physical/profile evidence; no inherited Fantasy d100 modifiers in another engine.

**Runtime:** Check physical feasibility, hands/readiness and shield-bearing arc; apply only one appropriate protection/stress mechanism.

**Portability:** Shield family is reusable in any context with a physical shield and compatible protection adapter.

**Unresolved:** construction/mass; handling/orientation; protected area; selected defense/stress profile

**Required qualification:** Trace each claim to source row and native-adapter boundary; Execute documented acceptance counterexample in at least two compatible contexts where relevant; Demonstrate unchanged current aliases and source payload hashes; Publish a new immutable per-definition revision before formally replacing the held gate

# PART II — 192 R2 proposed dossiers

R2 preserves native source evidence, contracts, prerequisites, negative effects, actual owners, and paired acceptance specifications. No R2 entry becomes selected merely by appearing here.

### SCL:PBC-BIO-FL-CRT-002@0.2 — Pack hunter

**Type:** behavior | **Status:** PROPOSED; source-backed hold remains

**Current Reference Gate:** source_backed_hold

**Source Field Status:** Source excerpts reproduced as received, not claims of scientific accuracy

**Source Field Status:** Source excerpts reproduced as received, not claims of scientific accuracy

**Definition:** Anatomy-dependent physical delivery method — Bite

**Feasibility Prerequisites:** Actual organism, relevant organ and position, action commitment, physical effect plus any separate exposure carrier

**Entry Specific Qualification:** Anatomical organ and positioned delivery for Bite

**Intended Effect Contract:** No attack, restraint, venom or free action without actual delivery and named species trait

**Negative Boundary:** A Bite descriptor does not automatically injure, grapple, poison or strike twice

**Actual Owner Interface:** K-ACTOR/K-POP

**No Duplicate Owner:** Use existing parent records for assets, stocks, personnel, time, messages, services and institutions; this reference cannot write those facts independently.

**Native Adapter Boundary:** Portability applies to content identity, interfaces, and qualitative constraints. No Fantasy Threat/Defeat, medicine effectiveness, injury timing, structural loading, power, heat or production rate is universalized.

**Positive Acceptance Fixture:** Give an existing creature the specified organ, effective position and action; check delivery before any separate toxic/exposure effect. For this family additionally verify: Anatomical organ and positioned delivery for Bite.

**Negative Acceptance Fixture:** If the organ cannot reach or delivery fails, no automatic grapple, venom or injury occurs. Family-specific non-effect: A Bite descriptor does not automatically injure, grapple, poison or strike twice.

---

# PART III — Final disposition

**No removals, promotions, or migration.** This package combines the complete Core 3.2 and SCL 1.0 normative specification with source-preserving review contracts. The full source set from Core v3.1 is preserved separately under `provenance/` for historical comparison.

Future adoption must publish new immutable family revisions and verify them under the then-active engine.
